Nó là **ƯỚC LƯỢNG\n+    ĐIỂM CÓ ĐIỀU KIỆN** vào một tham số mô hình **chưa được neo**: trần fill %ADV/phiên\n+    (`liquidity_volume_pct` = 0,20 = 20%ADV, đo được là **bind ~90% số phiên-fill**). Lý do bỏ\n+    nhãn cận dưới: tồn tại ĐÚNG HAI thiên lệch đã biết, **NGƯỢC CHIỀU, cùng bậc độ lớn**:\n+      (A) LÊN — L0 rót vốn vào nhóm mã live không mua được: **+4,08pp** (L0→L1 tại pct=0,20).\n+      (B) XUỐNG — cả hai chân giả định fill tới 20%ADV/phiên, trong khi fill THẬT của DNSE\n+          (T4, 07-01→08-03) chỉ xác nhận tới **3,86%ADV/phiên**: L1@0,20 → L1@0,05 = **−4,50pp**.\n+      Áp CẢ HAI: L1 @ pct=0,05 = **26,82%** vs chân đối chứng L0 @ pct=0,20 = **27,24%** ⇒\n+      **−0,42pp: hai thiên lệch GẦN TRIỆT TIÊU NHAU.** (Số của (A)/(B) đo ở cơ sở ADV `close`\n+      nên chân đối chứng là 27,24%; pin chính thức hiện hành 28,86% ở cơ sở `price` — kết luận\n+      về CHIỀU không phụ thuộc cơ sở giá, xem \"cơ sở giá\" dưới.)\n+    ⇒ **ĐỌC SỐ PIN KHÔNG THEO CHIỀU NÀO** — không cận dưới, không cận trên. Một con số \"~92% substitution, ~8% né lỗ\" từng xuất\n-    hiện ở bản docstring trước — KHÔNG có phép đo nào chống lưng, đã gỡ. Đầu mối mạnh nhất cho\n-    lần đo sau (quant-skeptic chỉ ra): TREAT vào lệnh nhiều hơn +30,1% (1.652→2.149) nhưng vị\n-    thế HOÀN TẤT lại ít hơn −16,3% (674→564), tỷ lệ ABANDONED_REFUND 59,2% (CTRL) → 73,8%\n-    (TREAT). RỦI RO CÒN MỞ, phải nói thẳng: \"vốn chảy sang event LAG kế tiếp\" và \"book đơn giản\n-    là KHÔNG fill nổi mã LAG ở quy mô 25B\" để lại CÙNG một dấu vết trên CSV — chưa tách được.\n-    Nếu vế sau đúng thì +4,11pp là hiện vật của mô hình fill chứ không phải edge thật, và việc\n-    mirror bộ lọc sang live sẽ không mang lại gì. Bộ lọc này vẫn ĐÚNG về mặt logic (không mua\n-    được thì đừng đặt mục tiêu), nhưng ĐỪNG dùng +4,11pp làm cơ sở kỳ vọng cho tới khi tách xong.\n+    ĐÃ TÁCH ĐƯỢC **CƠ CHẾ**, CHƯA HIỆU CHUẨN ĐƯỢC **MỨC** (cập nhật 2026-08-03 sau T1–T5; thay\n+    cho mục \"CHƯA PHÂN RÃ ĐƯỢC +4,11pp\" của bản trước):\n+      · Giả thuyết H_B — \"Δ là hiện vật capacity: sổ ở quy mô này đơn giản KHÔNG fill nổi mã LAG\"\n+        — **ĐÃ BỊ BÁC BỎ HAI LẦN bằng hai knob TRỰC GIAO, cả hai lần bằng SAI DẤU đạo hàm**:\n+        (T1) nới trần %ADV/phiên 20x ⇒ Δ **TĂNG** đơn điệu +2,51 → +4,08 → +5,20pp;\n+        (T2) hạ NAV 10x (50B→5B) ⇒ Δ đạt **CỰC ĐẠI +5,48pp ở đầu 5B**. H_B đòi Δ teo về ≤+0,5pp\n+        ở cả hai đầu lỏng. Manipulation check của cả hai thang đơn điệu đúng chiều (abandoned%\n+        L0 56,8→34,6% theo %ADV; 30,2→54,5% theo NAV) ⇒ can thiệp có ăn, phép thử không vô hiệu.\n+      · Cộng lập luận thẳng từ code: `liquidity_require_positive` **CHỈ** biến một `daily_max`\n+        VÔ HẠN thành 0, không bao giờ nới ⇒ L1 là **siết chặt nghiêm ngặt** của L0, và một hiện\n+        vật kiểu \"mô hình fill quá dễ dãi\" **không thể** sinh ra từ chân bị siết chặt hơn.\n+      · **Còn MỞ = MỨC.** Cả hai chân dùng chung một mô hình fill (%ADV/ngày) chưa neo vào fill\n+        thật; T4 đo được lệnh live chưa bao giờ vượt 3,86%ADV/phiên (cận dưới, mẫu chệch). ⇒\n+        không con số nào của A/B này được đọc như kỳ vọng live — xem \"NHÃN CỦA SỐ PIN\" ở trên.\n+      · Bằng chứng đầy đủ: `mike/agents/Taylor/research/lag_fidelity_decomp_20260803/`\n+        (README.md, T1/T2/T4_RESULTS.md, T5_DECISION.md).\n \n     ⚠️ KHE HỞ LIVE CHƯA BỊT (phát hiện cùng lúc, KHÔNG tự sửa — đổi luật sizing production\n     phải qua user): đường LIVE (script này + trading_bot/plan.py + strategies.py) KHÔNG có\n"}

Tức ở CẢ HAI cơ sở giá, áp đồng thời hai thiên lệch cho một số\n+      **cách chân pin < 0,5pp**, và **dấu của phần dư ĐỔI CHIỀU** (−0,42 → +0,32) ⇒ phần dư nằm\n+      trong nhiễu, không phải hiệu ứng có hướng. Also independently recomputed peak/day-count of the forced-borrow ledger for E125_f13: true simultaneous peak debt = 30.55B (not the reported 44.5B) and true distinct debt-days = 835 (not the reported 1,435) — did NOT match; see arithmetic_mechanism.", "killer_objection": "The report's own flagship evidence for 'leverage genuinely activated' (44.5B peak / 1,435 sessions, repeated verbatim in the finding's topic string) is computed by summing two per-book quantities that don't co-occur on the same date/day — an upper-bound artifact of `max(BAL)+max(LAG)` and `count(BAL)+count(LAG)`, not the actual simultaneous exposure. The corrected numbers (30.5B peak / 835 distinct days) still comfortably exceed p2's near-inert result, so the qualitative claim 'leverage is now real, not fake' survives — but the specific headline number cited as proof is wrong by 31-42% and should not be re-cited until fixed.", "recommended_reruns": ["Fix cau1_borrow_table.py / the engine's forced-borrow-audit print to aggregate BAL+LAG notional by calendar date FIRST, then take max() for peak debt, and use nunique(date) on the unioned day-set for 'phien co du no' — re-issue corrected CAU1 numbers (report should read ~30.5B/50B peak and 835 sessions, not 44.5B/1,435) before this finding is cited elsewhere in the fleet KB.", "Re-verify whether the same book-summation bug affects the per-f live-conversion table in §1.3 (the 'vay nang nhat %NAV' column) — that table is built per-event from `loan_vnd` (an entry-time snapshot, structurally different from the daily notional ledger), so it is likely unaffected, but should be explicitly confirmed rather than assumed."], "summary": "This is a well-run, mostly self-consistent piece of research: the core deliverable — that a genuinely-borrowed (not proxy) margin overlay on the CAPIT washout sleeve clears all four registered G-D gates (OOS/IS ΔCAGR positive, ΔMaxDD within +1.0pp budget, DSR≈1.0, PBO<0.5, no single event carrying ≥50% of the edge) at both the base 12.5% and adversarial 14% borrow rate, and that the modest +0.663pp full-period gain (vs. the proxy layer's overstated +1.82pp) is explained by capital dilution (leverage applies to only ~27% of one book, not the whole NAV) rather than edge destruction — reproduces exactly from the underlying logs and CSVs on independent recomputation, including the control's byte-level match to the official R3 pin, the INERT knob check, all four arithmetic-identity checks in the CAU2 decomposition, and the CAU3 correlation coefficients. However, the report's own showcase evidence that leverage is 'genuinely activated' this time (peak debt 44.5B/50B and 1,435 debt-sessions) does not survive independent recomputation from the same CSVs the report cites: both numbers are artifacts of summing two books' independently-timed peaks/day-counts instead of aggregating by date first, overstating the true simultaneous peak (30.5B) and true distinct debt-day count (835) by roughly a third to a half. This does not overturn the gate-PASS conclusion or the dilution mechanism, which I verified independently of that flawed table, but it is a real, reproducible defect in the finding's flagship evidentiary claim and should be corrected — and treated as a caution against taking headline numbers in this report family at face value without re-deriving them — before this is cited further as 'confirmed real leverage' anywhere in the fleet KB. The report is appropriately conservative everywhere else (explicitly not a wire proposal, discloses the live-conversion numbers as a lower bound, discloses an unexplained f=1.1/1.2 dilution-model gap rather than papering over it), which is why I'm not downgrading to REFUTED, but confidence is medium rather than high given the one confirmed arithmetic miss in central evidence."}

Also independently grepped the entire repo (outside the selfcheck's own fixture writer) for any code path that could write capit_margin_lever.enabled=true — found none, confirming claim (2). Confirmed data/trading_rules.json is genuinely gitignored via `git check-ignore -v` (matches `*.json` pattern at .gitignore:12), consistent with the 'no diff/blame trail on the JSON switch, scope must live in code' argument used to justify the APPROVED_* constants.", "killer_objection": "Every check here runs against fixtures/mocks (_FakeDNSEClient, synthetic dd52=-25.0, PaperBroker) because the real dd52 gate has never fired live — the feature's docstring itself admits this ('dd52 hiện tại còn xa −20%, nên đường đi của cờ vay chưa từng chạy sống một lần nào'). If DNSE's real loan_packages API response shape or loanPackageId field naming differs even slightly from what the fixtures assume, both _validate_lever_package (the resolver) and _lever_package_audit (the safety net) share that same assumption and could silently agree with each other while being wrong together on the very first live trigger — the two layers are not independent verification of the real API contract, only of each other's logic.", "recommended_reruns": ["When dd52 next approaches -20% in production monitoring, do a live-but-dry-run test against the real DNSE loan_packages endpoint (read-only GET) to confirm the actual JSON schema matches what _FakeDNSEClient/_resolve_loan_package_id assume, before the feature is ever flipped to enabled=true.", "Commit the mike/bin/send_plan_report.sh F3 change in the nested mike/ repo (currently shows as uncommitted 'M bin/send_plan_report.sh') so the fix has a permanent audit trail before it's relied on in a real approval flow."], "summary": "I traced every artifact the finding cites — capit_lever_policy()/the CAPIT_LEVER_BEGIN/END block in golive_recommend_v23.py, apply_capit_lever() in trading_bot/plan.py, the broker-layer package validation in brokers.py, the runtime _lever_package_audit() guard in executor.py, and the WARN-gate patch in mike/bin/send_plan_report.sh — and all match the claimed approved scope (f=1.3, dd52<=-20%, loan package 1840 'RocketX', SpaceX-only, CAPIT-buy-only) exactly, with the APPROVED_* constants pinned in version-controlled code rather than the gitignored JSON. I independently reran the 88-check selfcheck across four TZ environments (all 88/0), reran the six peer selfchecks (all pass), and ran shellcheck on the shell script (exit 0) — none of this was taken on faith. I also independently confirmed there is no code path anywhere in the repo that could flip capit_margin_lever.enabled to true besides a manual edit of data/trading_rules.json, and confirmed the F3 fix to the WARN gate is a small, surgical diff that preserves the 07-21 sizing-error detection while correctly rebasing the comparison when leverage is active. The only real residual risk — not something this round of fixes could have addressed — is that the whole chain has only ever been exercised against fixtures/mocks, since the dd52 gate has never fired live; the first genuine trigger is the true test of the DNSE API contract assumptions shared by both the resolver and its own safety net."}

- [2026-08-03T14:41:39Z] quant-skeptic/verification — ✅ CONFIRMED VERIFY: ad-hoc claim: {"finding_topic": "ad-hoc claim", "verdict": "CONFIRMED", "confidence": "medium", "checks": {"look_ahead_leak": "na — this is an operational wiring/authorization claim, not a signal; dd52 uses only trailing Close (rolling-252d max), no forward columns touched", "oos_robustness": "na — no performance/edge claim is made ('KHÔNG tuyên bố gì về lợi nhuận/edge'); OOS validity of the underlying p1-p5 research is a separate finding already reviewed elsewhere", "panel_curation_bias": "na — no universe/backtest claim in scope", "reproducibility_selfcheck": "pass — ran capit_lever_selfcheck.py myself: 96/96 PASS, 0 FAIL, reproduced identically under TZ=Asia/Ho_Chi_Minh, TZ=America/New_York, and `env -u TZ`. Also ran 5 of the cited regression selfchecks (churn_guard, extreme_regime, ghost_order, t2_settlement, tick_retry) independently: all rc=0. Could not locate a 7th/8th regression selfcheck to match the claimed '7 selfcheck regression' count — minor unverified sub-claim, not material to the wiring/scope thesis.", "param_overfit": "na — f=1.3/package 1840/dd52<=-20% are cited as user-approved fixed parameters from the already-reviewed p1-p5 chain, not re-tuned here; this finding only checks that the wired code matches those exact values (it does)", "capacity_adv_realism": "pass — code comment and cascade order confirm capit_adv_caps (%ADV cap) is applied BEFORE apply_capit_lever and is explicitly NOT multiplied by f ('Trần %ADV capit_adv_caps KHÔNG nhân f'); leverage cannot be used to route around the market-impact cap", "arithmetic_mechanism": "pass, with one caveat — I independently re-ran the exact round-1 exploit scenario (arch-reviewer's '10 tỷ trên mốc 325 triệu' probe) directly against the live trading_bot/plan.py::apply_capit_lever (not the selfcheck's mocked harness): crafted an artifact with capit_slot_target_vnd_levered=10,000,000,000 VND while base capit_slot_target_vnd stayed honest at 50,000,000 VND and f=1.3 (in-envelope). Result: order was DENIED, lever_f/loan_package_id came back None, reason correctly cited the anchored cap (71,500,000 VND = base×1.3×1.10 tolerance) — the round-1 hole is genuinely closed in the real code path, not just in the selfcheck fixture."}, "independent_recompute": "Reproduced two headline numbers independently: (1) capit_lever_selfcheck.py 96/96 PASS across 3 TZ environments — exact match to the claimed count; (2) hand-built a malicious golive_v23_status.json (inflated *_levered VND fields, honest base field, in-envelope f/package) and called trading_bot.plan.apply_capit_lever() directly — got the same DENIED/anchored-cap outcome the finding claims, confirming the fix lives in the real execution-tier code, not only in test doubles. Also confirmed via `git check-ignore` that both data/trading_rules.json and data/golive_v23_status.json are gitignored (*.json, .gitignore:12) with empty `git log` history — validates the stated rationale for why scope must be pinned in code (trading_bot/plan.py CAPIT_LEVER_APPROVED_F/PACKAGE/ACCOUNTS) rather than trusted from JSON alone. Confirmed current on-disk trading_rules.json has capit_margin_lever.enabled=false, and grep shows the single write path for o.lever_f/o.loan_package_id is exclusively inside apply_capit_lever() — no other code sets these fields, so the enabled=false → zero-behavior-change claim holds structurally, not just by test assertion.", "killer_objection": "The anchored VND cap only closes the specific round-1 exploit (editing the *_levered field alone). It does NOT independently verify the base field (capit_slot_target_vnd / capit_total_target_vnd) itself at execution time — apply_capit_lever() trusts whatever is on disk in the gitignored, unsigned golive_v23_status.json for that base. The code's own comment concedes this is only a *detective* control ('sửa nó cũng làm sai lệch mọi con số CAPIT khác... sẽ bị phát hiện ở cổng WARN 07-21 của send_plan_report.sh'), and that WARN gate runs once at plan-APPROVAL time (~21:00), not at execution time (~09:05 next day) — there is a ~12h window where an artifact edit to BOTH base and levered fields consistently (still 'f=1.3, in envelope') would sail through the anchored-cap check with no runtime cross-check against an independent source (e.g. a live BQ/NAV recompute, or a hash/signature pinned at publish time). So the finding's strongest phrasing — 'một artifact bị sửa tay không thể tự nâng hạn mức vay' — is true only for the levered field in isolation, not for a two-field edit of the same artifact. This doesn't fabricate a new hole (it's a narrower version of the same root cause the finding is fixing), but it means the fix is a mitigation, not a closure, of hand-edited-artifact risk.", "recommended_reruns": ["Add a capit_lever_selfcheck.py case that tampers with BOTH capit_slot_target_vnd (base) and capit_slot_target_vnd_levered consistently (e.g. base×3, levered=base×3×1.3) and confirms whether the current code still authorizes the inflated loan — expected to currently PASS the (flawed) anchor check, which would demonstrate the residual gap directly", "Locate/confirm the remaining regression selfchecks to substantiate the '7 selfcheck regression rc=0' claim precisely (only 5 were found and independently re-run here: churn_guard, extreme_regime, ghost_order, t2_settlement, tick_retry)", "Consider adding an execution-time integrity check on data/golive_v23_status.json (hash/signature stamped at publish ~19:03, verified at execution ~09:05) or a live BQ recompute of capit_total_target_vnd as a second independent anchor, closing the base-field trust gap noted above"], "summary": "This is a wiring/scope claim, not a performance claim, and it holds up under attack. I independently reproduced the 96/96 selfcheck pass across three timezones, confirmed the single write-path for lever_f/loan_package_id lives exclusively in trading_bot/plan.py::apply_capit_lever(), confirmed data/trading_rules.json on disk today has capit_margin_lever.enabled=false (so zero live behavior change vs V2.4 holds structurally), confirmed the approved scope (f=1.3, package 1840 'RocketX', capit_only, SpaceX-only) is pinned as code constants imported (not re-declared) by both the signal-publishing tier and the execution tier, and — most importantly — I independently re-ran the exact round-1 arch-reviewer exploit (artifact claiming a 10-billion-VND loan cap on a 325-million-VND base) directly against the real apply_capit_lever() function outside the selfcheck harness, and it correctly denied the order and stripped the leverage flags. The one genuine gap this review surfaces, which the finding's own code comments quietly acknowledge but the finding's headline phrasing overstates, is that the anchored cap trusts the artifact's UNLEVERED base field with no independent runtime verification — an attacker (or corrupted process) able to hand-edit the gitignored golive_v23_status.json could inflate both the base and levered fields together and still pass the f-envelope and anchor checks, since the only cross-check on the base field is a human-facing WARN at plan-approval time, not a runtime re-verification at execution time. Section B2/B4 proposes reusing this exact cap verbatim as the live per-session trim ceiling ('không đặt trần %/phiên mới... `_etf_day_cap` đã có sẵn') while simultaneously acknowledging (B4) that live PARK is '30 mã riêng lẻ' vs the engine's pooled-ETF abstraction — but never reconciles that the liquidity ceiling itself, not just the trim ORDER, was pooled-ETF-shaped. Applying an ETF-ADV-sized VND cap to trims spread across up to 30 individual (some potentially illiquid) value names risks breaching per-name %ADV even while staying under the aggregate cap.", "arithmetic_mechanism": "pass — verified the '6/13 years C beats A' sub-claim by hand from the raw annual tables: C > A in exactly 2016, 2017, 2019, 2020, 2022, 2025 (6 years) — exact match to the doc's named years. `w_lag_current=0.495`/`alloc_note='as of 2026-07-31'`/`band_breach=false`/`n_park=30`/`park_rebal_date=2026-05-05`/`etf_park_frac=0.7` all reproduced byte-for-byte from `data/golive_v23_status.json`. `deferred_total_with_fee_vnd=171,096,626`, `available_cash_vnd=4,821,143`, `orders=[]` and the literal deferred_reason text reproduced from `data/trade_plans/plan_SpaceX_2026-08-04.json`. `totalCash=14,596,244` reproduced from `dnse_raw_2026-08-03.jsonl`. Mechanism claims (allocator is post-hoc scalar accounting, not order-generating; DT5G is untouched by this whole topic) are consistent with code."}, "independent_recompute": "Recomputed IS/OOS geometric CAGR from the raw per-year sys-return series in v2_A_control.log and v2_C_skip.log: got A_IS=27.04%, C_IS=25.43%, A_OOS=30.15%, C_OOS=30.29% — matches the finding's C3 table to the basis point. Also reproduced md5 clustering (v1 3-way tie, v2 3-way distinct with A==production), the 6/13-year win count, and every cited field in golive_v23_status.json / plan_SpaceX_2026-08-04.json / dnse_raw_2026-08-03.jsonl exactly.", "killer_objection": "The proposed L1/L2 trim cap (`_etf_day_cap`) is derived from E1VFVN30 ETF ADV, not from the ADV of the 30 individual custom30V names that live would actually be trimming — the design reuses a pooled-liquidity number for a fundamentally different (per-name, possibly less liquid) execution surface without checking the reconciliation. This is a real, unaddressed capacity risk in section B (not yet wired), distinct from the otherwise well-substantiated diagnostic finding.", "recommended_reruns": ["Before implementing L1/L2: pull per-ticker `Trading_Value_1M_P50`/ADV for the current 30-name custom30V basket and compare against the VND size a single day's `_etf_day_cap()` would authorize a trim of, split pro-rata across names — confirm no single name's implied trim exceeds a sane %ADV.", "Run the promised 4th ablation leg (disable block 4c / PREFILL_STATE_REBAL) to isolate L1's standalone contribution, as the doc itself flags as outstanding.", "Complete the P0 book/play_type-at-fill journal tagging (A7 blocker) before computing any live `park_mv_live`, since the current name-based inference is already known to misclassify at least one live position (VPB tagged 'LAG/PARK')."], "summary": "This is an unusually well-evidenced finding — I independently retraced nearly every specific claim (code line numbers, md5 hashes, JSON field values, DNSE balance figures, plan-file deferred amounts, and a from-scratch IS/OOS CAGR recompute from the raw annual-return logs) and every one matched exactly, including the self-critical parts (the v1-ablation-was-a-no-op correction, and the OOS-near-zero/LOO-2023-carries-it-all caveat that correctly bars citing +0.78pp as alpha). The core diagnosis — live implements only the buy-side of a bidirectional PARK mechanism, creating a ratchet, and is provably breaching its own backtested 70/30 parking rule today (182.5tr over cap vs 171.1tr of deferred buy need) — is solid and the ablation is honestly caveated rather than oversold. The one real gap is in the not-yet-implemented design (section B): it proposes reusing the engine's `_etf_day_cap`, which is sized to the E1VFVN30 ETF's own liquidity, as the live trim ceiling for 30 discrete individual stocks — a capacity mismatch the doc doesn't reconcile despite otherwise being very careful about not introducing new parameters. Nothing has been wired to production; this review should gate on fixing that capacity check (and the acknowledged P0 book-tagging blocker) before any code change to `golive_recommend_v23.py` or plan generation."}

This doesn't break the fix (the new logic never reads created_at at all, so it's correct regardless of whether some historical plans populated it), and the underlying doc (§12 of margin_kelly_production_wiring_20260803.md) is more careful, scoping its claim to the specific recent files it actually checked (08-04, 08-03, 07-29, ZaloPay) rather than asserting a universal zero. I independently verified every material claim: the self-check runs 182/182 PASS across five timezone configurations exactly as claimed; reverting plan.py alone via git stash while keeping the new test file reproduces the exact claimed 3-test failure (J15b, J15d, J20) proving the tests are real regression tests and not tautologies; the 6 cited unrelated regression selfchecks all pass with rc=0, showing no collateral breakage; the single caller of the changed function is contained to lever_live_preflight; the second-gate mitigation for the disclosed residual risk (qty-only changes on a reused id) genuinely exists in the code; and enabled remains false in trading_rules.json so this ships with zero live-trading behavior change. The one soft spot is that the finding's compressed 'plan_files_with_created_at: 0' claim is a slight overstatement of an otherwise well-scoped and correctly-argued underlying doc — a handful of older plan files (pre 2026-07-06) did have the field populated — but this doesn't undermine the fix's correctness since the new logic doesn't depend on created_at at all, and the finding transparently discloses its own remaining limitations (#1 and #8) rather than hiding them. This is a well-evidenced, reproducible, low-risk fix to a real correctness bug in still-disabled code."}
